---
title: "Source field dictionary"
description: "974 source-normalized definitions with API types, presence, nullability, provenance and coverage rules."
keywords: ["AMLOK", "data model", "API contract"]
---

These are the exact source-normalized fields returned inside `DataRecord.normalized` and `Evidence.normalized`. The [OpenAPI contract](/openapi/amlok.json) defines their wire schemas; the [API correspondence](/data-model/api-alignment) separates these records from public product resources and research concepts.

**Required** means present within its containing object. A nested required field can still be absent when its parent is optional. **Nullable** means JSON null is accepted; omission and null are different. Missing or restricted evidence never becomes zero, false or a completed no-hit result. Source records can be partial; inspect `data_status` and `missing_fields`.

Resource prefixes are dictionary context. `bank_transaction.amount.amount` is `data.normalized.amount.amount` on Get data record. Arrays use `[]` in dictionary paths. Common fields live at that same normalized object level, not under a `common` property. The detailed `verification_assessment` resource has its own [verification field dictionary](/api-reference/verification-fields), including its metadata.

Download the [machine-readable source dictionary](/downloads/data-model-field-catalog.json). It includes schema locations and the contract fingerprint used by console Data Lookup. Shared nested types resolve through [shared types](/data-model/shared-types) and OpenAPI.

## Platform

<Accordion title="common" id="common" description="16 fields">

#### `common.id`

**F0001** · `string` · Required · Nullable: No

Stable identifier scoped to the client tenant. Never use a provider ID as the public primary key.

- **Origin:** AMLOK managed
- **Classification:** Confidential
- **Availability:** Required when resource exists
- **Normalization / validation:** Never use a provider ID as the public primary key.
- **Source families:** AMLOK
- **Presence:** Required within its containing object. All containing objects are required.
- **Schema:** NormalizedConnectionRead/properties/id

#### `common.object`

**F0002** · `string` · Required · Nullable: No

- **Origin:** AMLOK managed
- **Classification:** Confidential
- **Availability:** Required when resource exists
- **Normalization / validation:** Preserve original evidence; do not invent missing values.
- **Source families:** AMLOK

## Financial supporting schedule fields

`financial_statement.supporting_schedule` contains the following optional nullable decimal-string inputs. Amounts use the parent statement currency and reporting period. Missing fields are unknown, not zero. Opening balances mean the start of that period; closing balances mean its end. Exact formulas and eligibility are in [risk-package methodology](/api-reference/risk-package-methodology). The same nested fields are searchable in Console Data Lookup.

| Field | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `supporting_schedule.revenue` | Revenue. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.prior_year_revenue` | Prior year revenue. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.cogs` | Cogs. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.payroll` | Payroll. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.prior_year_payroll` | Prior year payroll. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.other_operating_expenses` | Other operating expenses. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.depreciation` | Depreciation. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.interest` | Interest. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.tax` | Tax. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.net_profit` | Net profit. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
| `supporting_schedule.cash` | Cash. Source-supported numeric schedule input; money uses the parent statement currency and period. Count/day/ratio inputs use the named unit. Missing is null or omitted, never an assumed zero. |
